Abstract: Quadruped robots are increasingly expected to navigate through narrow passages, cluttered indoor scenes, and large-scale 3D unstructured environments. Existing local planners commonly approximate the robot using isotropic geometric inflation or rely on planar and elevation-map representations, leading to conservative motion in tight spaces and limited reasoning about overhanging structures. This letter presents SCAN-Planner, a spatial collision-aware local planning framework for long-range quadruped navigation. A yaw-aware twin-cylinder footprint is used to model the elongated robot body, enabling whole-body collision evaluation through sparse queries in an inflated 3D occupancy map. We further introduce a projected A* search that generates collision-free guidance on an interpolated ground-following surface, with z-gradient suppression to avoid obstacles horizontally while maintaining vertical stability. For large-scale deployment, a robot-centric sliding map with boundary fallback provides high-resolution local collision checking and recovery from local dead ends. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that SCAN-Planner generates safe, smooth, and efficient trajectories in dense clutter, 3D unstructured scenes, stair traversal, and long-range navigation tasks.

</details>


### [111] [pdSTL: Probabilistic Differentiable Signal Temporal Logic for Stochastic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19561)
*Bennett Dogbey,Hemanth Manjunatha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 pdSTL（概率可微分信号时序逻辑），一种统一概率语义与可微分鲁棒性的框架，用于在信念轨迹上计算保守满意度边界，并通过 LSTM 式展开实现线性时间可微分监控，用于端到端轨迹优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人在不确定环境中需满足复杂时序与安全规约，现有 STL 方法要么不可微分，要么忽略信念空间的不确定性，缺乏兼具概率保证与可微优化能力的统一框架。

Method: 构建区间值概率语义计算保守满意度边界，通过 STL 语法树组合传播；将时序鲁棒性评估设计为 LSTM 风格循环展开，实现线性时间可微分监控。

Result: 在仿真障碍物避让、换道场景以及真实 Crazyflie 四旋翼飞行实验中验证，pdSTL 在保持安全裕度方面显著优于确定性可微分 STL，并提供形式化概率保证。

Conclusion: pdSTL 是首个兼具概率语义与可微性的 STL 框架，可用于不确定环境下的安全关键轨迹优化，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in uncertain environments must satisfy complex temporal and safety specifications despite stochastic dynamics and sensing noise. While Signal Temporal Logic (STL) offers robustness measures for gradient-based optimization, existing extensions either lack differentiability or ignore belief-space uncertainty. We introduce pdSTL (probabilistic differentiable Signal Temporal Logic), a framework that unifies probabilistic semantics with differentiable robustness over belief trajectories. pdSTL employs interval-valued probabilistic semantics to compute conservative satisfaction bounds, propagated compositionally through the STL syntax tree. We formulate the temporal robustness evaluation as a recurrent, LSTM-style unfolding of STL operators, enabling linear-time, differentiable monitoring suitable for end-to-end trajectory optimization. We validate pdSTL on simulated obstacle avoidance, lane-change maneuvers, and real-world Crazyflie quadcopter flight experiments under aerodynamic disturbances. Results demonstrate that pdSTL achieves efficient optimization with formal probabilistic guarantees, significantly outperforming deterministic differentiable STL in maintaining safety margins under real-world uncertainty.

</details>


### [112] [One Demo is Worth a Thousand Trajectories: Action-View Augmentation for Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19586)
*Chuer Pan,Litian Liang,Dominik Bauer,Eric Cousineau,Benjamin Burchfiel,Siyuan Feng,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对鱼眼相机的3D高斯溅射数据增强框架，从真实眼在手演示中生成视觉逼真的鱼眼图像序列和物理可行的动作轨迹，提升机械臂操作策略在分布外场景（如障碍物）中的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略对机器人初始配置和未见障碍物敏感，微小变化即导致分布外观测，需大量数据收集才能避免失败。现有数据增强方法难以生成物理可行且视觉逼真的鱼眼图像-动作对。

Method: 1) 提出适配广角鱼眼相机的3D高斯溅射（Gaussian Splatting）重建和编辑3D场景，可插入未见物体；2) 利用轨迹优化生成平滑、无碰撞、利于渲染新视角的动作轨迹；3) 从新视角渲染鱼眼观测图像，形成增强数据对。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，该方法在相同场景和含有障碍物的增强场景中均显著提升了多种操作任务的成功率。

Conclusion: 基于鱼眼高斯溅射的数据增强框架是提升操作策略泛化性、应对分布外场景的有效手段。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies for manipulation have demonstrated remarkable potential in modeling complex robotic behaviors, yet minor alterations in the robot's initial configuration and unseen obstacles easily lead to out-of-distribution observations. Without extensive data collection effort, these result in catastrophic execution failures. In this work, we introduce an effective data augmentation framework that generates visually realistic fisheye image sequences and corresponding physically feasible action trajectories from real-world eye-in-hand demonstrations, captured with a portable parallel gripper with a single fisheye camera. We introduce a novel Gaussian Splatting formulation, adapted to wide FoV fisheye cameras, to reconstruct and edit the 3D scene with unseen objects. We utilize trajectory optimization to generate smooth, collision-free, view-rendering-friendly action trajectories and render visual observations from corresponding novel views. Comprehensive experiments in simulation and the real world show that our augmentation framework improves the success rate for various manipulation tasks in both the same scene and the augmented scene with obstacles requiring collision avoidance.

</details>


### [113] [Safe, Real-Time Active Model Discrimination and Fault Diagnosis for Nonlinear Systems via Differentiable Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19590)
*Xinpei Ni,Melkior Ornik,Glen Chou,Samuel Coogan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向不确定连续非线性系统的安全实时主动故障诊断算法，通过区间过近似的可达集分析和可微优化目标，实现传感器与执行器故障的确定性诊断，并在多种机器人平台上验证了50ms内的高效可靠诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动故障诊断方法在处理不确定非线性系统时难以同时保证安全约束和实时性，且缺乏对传感器/执行器多种故障模式的统一框架。

Method: 将故障诊断建模为输出反馈时变策略优化问题，用区间过近似描述可达状态/输出集，设计可微目标函数惩罚候选模型可达输出集的重叠区域，利用JAX和可微可达性原语实现梯度优化求解。

Result: 在四旋翼、战斗机模型、差分驱动机器人和四足机器人上，支持最多11种故障模式，诊断时间<50ms，诊断成功率优于基线方法，并提供形式化安全保障。

Conclusion: 该方法为非线性系统的安全实时主动故障诊断提供了一种高效且可扩展的解决方案，兼具理论保证和实际部署可行性。

Abstract: We present a safe, real-time algorithm for active fault diagnosis and model discrimination for uncertain continuous-time nonlinear systems with process and measurement disturbances. Given a finite set of candidate models representing nominal and faulty modes, including actuator and sensor faults, we formulate an output-feedback, time-varying policy optimization problem that (i) robustly enforces state-input safety constraints over a finite horizon and (ii) drives the system to produce sampled measurements consistent with at most one model, enabling deterministic diagnosis. To solve this problem in real time, we develop a tractable approximation using interval over-approximations of reachable state and output sets, and encode diagnosability via a differentiable objective that penalizes overlap between the reachable output sets of possible models. The resulting optimization is solved efficiently online with gradient-based methods using JAX and differentiable reachability primitives. We evaluate our method on sensor and actuator fault diagnosis (up to 11 fault modes) in several high-dimensional nonlinear robotic systems, including a simulated quadrotor and fighter-jet model, a hardware differential-drive robot, and quadrupedal navigation. Across these case studies, our approach achieves reliable model discrimination in under 50 ms, outperforming baselines in discrimination success rate and speed while providing formal safety guarantees.

</details>


### [114] [Fail-RAG : A Retrieval Augmented Generation Informed Framework for Robot Failure Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19598)
*Ameya Salvi,Jie Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Fail-RAG，一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的机器人操作故障检测框架，通过将故障图像和上下文信息嵌入并与故障数据库进行相似性查询，结合视觉语言模型（VLM）进行分析。在仓库自动化的多种任务中，相比现成VLM，故障检测准确率平均提升25个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库中用于物料搬运的机器人会遇到各种意外事件（故障），基于规则的检测方法难以应对动态环境和任务变化导致的故障形式变化，需要更灵活、可泛化的故障检测方法。

Method: 构建故障数据库，将故障图像和上下文信息嵌入为向量；通过相似性检索从数据库中查询相关故障；使用VLM按照指令模板对检索到的故障进行详细分析。

Result: 在五种机器人操作类型上，Fail-RAG的故障检测准确率平均比现成VLM高25个百分点。在固定机械臂和移动机械手上均进行了仿真和物理实验验证。

Conclusion: RAG框架能有效增强VLM在机器人故障检测中的表现，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Industry automation is witnessing an evolution in robotics driven by both technological breakthroughs and societal changes: progress towards generalist robots, embodied and physical artificial intelligence (AI), and increasing labor shortage in manufacturing.An intelligent autonomous robot needs to not only act according to planned motions but also react to any unexpected events. In this study, we focus on such unexpected events in warehouses where robots are used for material handling. Specifically, we refer to any unexpected events as failures and develop methods to detect robot operations related failures. Rule-based detection methods may break since the form of failures could change due to the dynamic nature of both environments and tasks. We propose 'Fail-RAG', a Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG)-based failure detection framework where failure images and context information are embedded and queried against a failure database by calculating their similarities. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are further used to analyze failures and provide details by following our instruction template. We evaluated the performance of Fail-RAG by conducting both simulation and physical experiments using fixed robot arms and a mobile manipulator for multiple tasks that are common in warehouse automation. Fail-RAG achieved 25 percentage point higher failure detection accuracy on average across five types of robot operations compared to using off-the-shelf VLMs, indicating its effectiveness for real-world failure detection.

</details>


### [115] [Formal Verification of Learned Multi-Agent Communication Policies via Decision Tree Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19632)
*Ahmad Farooq,Kamran Iqbal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个面向多智能体强化学习通信策略的安全验证框架，通过策略抽象（将神经策略蒸馏为决策树）实现形式化验证，并在多无人机编队场景中验证了安全属性可迁移至原始网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习在无人机群、自动驾驶车队等安全关键场景中缺乏形式化安全保证，现有神经策略不可解释且无法验证。

Method: 四阶段流水线：领域特征提取 → 决策树蒸馏（97.9%保真度）→ 自动翻译为PRISM概率模型检验规范 → 通过成对分解与联合界聚合对PCTL属性进行组合验证。

Result: 在5-7智能体多无人机编队中验证了18个时序逻辑属性，88.9%满足率，所有5项安全阈值均达标（碰撞概率0.3% vs 1%阈值），蒙特卡洛验证偏差≤0.6百分点，离散VQ-VIB通信相比连续方法保真度高11.6-13.6百分点，验证速度提升3-4倍。

Conclusion: 为深度MARL与形式化安全工作流之间搭建了实用桥梁，提供了经验验证的安全验证方法。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables agents to develop coordination strategies through emergent communication, but neural policies lack the formal safety guarantees required for safety-critical robotic deployment in drone swarms and autonomous vehicle fleets. We present the first end-to-end framework for safety verification of learned multi-agent communication policies through policy abstraction: neural policies are distilled into interpretable decision trees, then formally verified, with empirical validation confirming that verified safety properties transfer to original networks. Our four-stage pipeline consists of domain-specific feature extraction from agent observations, decision tree distillation achieving 97.9% +/- 1.2% fidelity to neural policies, automated translation to PRISM probabilistic model checker specifications with complete feature-to-state-variable correspondence, and compositional verification of Probabilistic Computation Tree Logic (PCTL) properties via pairwise decomposition with union-bound aggregation and empirical neighbor modeling. Evaluating Vector-Quantized Variational Information Bottleneck (VQ-VIB) policies for multi-drone coordination with 5-7 agents, we verify 18 temporal logic properties across safety, liveness, and cooperation, achieving 88.9% property satisfaction with all five safety thresholds satisfied (0.3% collision probability vs. 1% threshold). Monte Carlo validation of original neural policies confirms that verified safety properties transfer with <=0.6 percentage-point deviation (95% CI). Discrete VQ-VIB messages provide +11.6 to +13.6 percentage-point fidelity advantages over continuous methods, enabling 3-4x faster verification. Our framework provides empirically validated safety verification for distilled policy abstractions, serving as a practical bridge between deep MARL and formal safety workflows for multi-robot deployment.

</details>


### [116] [CTS-MoE: Implicit Terrain Adaptation via Mixture-of-Experts for Perceptive Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19633)
*Francisco Affonso,Matheus P. Angarola,Ana Luiza Mineiro,Aditya Potnis,Marcelo Becker,Girish Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CTS-MoE，一种基于密集混合专家actor与基于感知的门控机制的组合方法，通过多critic架构解决多任务强化学习中足式 locomotion 在非连续地形上的共享与分离矛盾，实现端到端训练并仅依赖感知进行部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人在楼梯、间隙、障碍等非连续地形上需要自适应行为，单一保守步态无法应对突变的拓扑变化。多任务强化学习中任务共享与分离之间存在张力：共用运动基座但奖励冲突，需要策略共享行为同时避免价值干扰。现有工作要么是单体策略牺牲专化，要么是层次化策略牺牲泛化能力。

Method: CTS-MoE包含：(1) 密集混合专家actor + 基于感知的门控，用于组合共享行为；(2) 多critic结构，每个任务独立价值头防止干扰；(3) 单阶段并发教师-学生训练框架，处理部分可观测性，避免顺序蒸馏；训练时使用任务标签，部署时仅依赖感知路由。

Result: 在宇树Go1机器人上的仿真和实物实验中，在已知和未知地形上均实现了任务感知的专化，相比单体基线方法具有更低的跟踪误差和更高的成功率。

Conclusion: CTS-MoE通过MoE架构与多critic设计有效解决了多任务足式locomotion中的共享与分离问题，无需高层选择器或地形分类器，仅依赖感知即可适应不同地形。

Abstract: Perceptive legged locomotion over discontinuous terrain (e.g., stairs, gaps, and obstacles) requires adaptive behavior, as a single conservative gait cannot produce the anticipatory maneuvers needed for abrupt topology changes. Cast as multi-task reinforcement learning, this problem introduces a tension between sharing and separation. Tasks use a common locomotion base but have conflicting rewards, so a policy must share behavior while avoiding value interference. Prior work addresses only one side, with monolithic policies sacrificing specialization and hierarchical sub-policies sacrificing generalization across transitions and unseen terrain. We propose CTS-MoE, which combines a dense mixture-of-experts actor with perception-based gating to compose shared behaviors and a multi-critic with task-specific value heads to prevent interference. The model is trained end-to-end in a single-stage concurrent teacher-student setup that handles partial observability and avoids sequential distillation, with task labels used only during training. At deployment, routing depends solely on perception, allowing terrain adaptation without a high-level selector or terrain classifier. Experiments on a Unitree Go1 in simulation and on hardware across seen and unseen terrains show task-aware specialization, with lower tracking error and higher success rates than monolithic baselines. Project Website: https://cts-moe.github.io/ .

</details>


### [117] [Scaling Self-Play for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19641)
*Luke Rowe,Roger Girgis,Rodrigue de Schaetzen,Daphne Cornelisse,Alaap Grandhi,Felix Heide,Eugene Vinitsky,Christopher Pal,Liam Paull*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Gigapixel——一个高吞吐量批处理驾驶模拟器，支持从像素观察进行大规模自对弈训练，并通过自对弈DAgger蒸馏训练（从特权RL教师蒸馏）训练端到端驾驶策略，再通过轻量级感知适配迁移到真实世界，在HUGSIM和NAVSIM-v2基准上取得竞争性能，无需人类轨迹监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端驾驶模型依赖离线人类演示数据，状态覆盖有限且缺乏闭环反馈，导致部署时产生复合误差并对长尾交互脆弱。自对弈方法虽有前景但通常假设鸟瞰图（BEV）向量化观测，与直接处理传感器观测的端到端策略不兼容。

Method: 1) Gigapixel：高吞吐量批处理驾驶模拟器，渲染简化包围盒世界（而非逼真传感器模拟），吞吐量达50k agent steps/s；2) 自对弈DAgger训练：从特权RL教师进行在线策略蒸馏训练像素级策略；3) 轻量级感知适配弥合sim-to-real差距。

Result: 在HUGSIM和NAVSIM-v2基准上取得竞争性能，无需人类轨迹监督；自对弈规模扩大带来性能成比例提升。

Conclusion: 自对弈是一种实用且可扩展的端到端驾驶模型训练策略，像素级自对弈训练可通过蒸馏和感知适配有效迁移到真实世界。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models are typically trained on offline human-demonstration datasets that provide limited state coverage and often no closed-loop feedback, making them prone to compounding errors when deployed in closed-loop and brittle to long-tail agent interactions. To overcome these limitations, we propose an alternative strategy for training end-to-end driving models: large-scale self-play directly from pixels in simulation. While prior self-play approaches have shown promising transfer to real-world driving, they typically assume vectorized Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) observations that are incompatible with end-to-end policies operating directly on sensor observations. To this end, we introduce Gigapixel, a high-throughput batched driving simulator with perspective rendering, enabling scalable self-play directly from pixel observations. Rather than targeting compute-costly photorealistic sensor simulation, Gigapixel renders a simplified bounding-box world that preserves essential scene structure while achieving throughput at 50k agent steps per second. Since direct pixel-space self-play RL is prohibitively sample-inefficient at end-to-end model scale, we propose self-play DAgger training: we train pixel-based policies in self-play via on-policy distillation from a privileged RL teacher. To bridge the sim-to-real gap, we subsequently transfer the self-play trained policies to real-world sensor data through lightweight perception adaptation. Policies trained in Gigapixel and adapted to real-world sensor data achieve competitive performance on the HUGSIM and NAVSIM-v2 benchmarks without human trajectory supervision. Moreover, scaling self-play training yields proportional gains in policy performance, establishing self-play as a practical and scalable strategy for training end-to-end models.

</details>


### [118] [DF-ExpEnse: Diffusion Filtered Exploration for Sample Efficient Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19656)
*Calvin Luo,Chen Sun,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DF-ExpEnse是一种利用预训练生成控制策略的多模态建模能力构建候选集，并通过集成评论家选择高质量与高探索兴趣动作的探索技术，可无缝集成到强化学习微调流程中，在操控和 locomotion 任务上显著提升微调样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的生成控制策略在离线经验上表现良好，但在线经验收集的质量影响微调样本效率，现有探索策略未充分利用生成策略的多模态特性。

Method: 利用生成控制策略的多模态建模创建表达力强且可评估的候选集，通过集成评论家（critic ensemble）选择平衡质量与探索兴趣的动作，在集群场景中支持跨智能体通信协作探索。

Result: 在多种操控和 locomotion 任务上，相比默认微调和替代动作选择方案，DF-ExpEnse 一致地提升了样本效率。

Conclusion: DF-ExpEnse是一种即插即用的探索模块，能有效提升预训练策略在线微调的样本效率，支持多智能体协作探索。

Abstract: A natural recipe for intelligent robotic decision-making is initializing from pretrained generative control policies, which have summarized offline experience, and adapting them to self-collected online experience. We present DF-ExpEnse, an exploration technique that improves the quality of online experience collection, thus increasing finetuning sample-efficiency. DF-ExpEnse leverages the multimodal modeling capabilities of the generative control policy to create an expressive and tractably evaluatable candidate set. It then utilizes an ensemble of critics to identify the action that best balances quality with high exploration interest. In fleet settings, DF-ExpEnse further enables cross-agent communication to facilitate collaborative exploration as a group. DF-ExpEnse can be seamlessly integrated with existing strategies that finetune pretrained generative control policies via reinforcement learning. We experimentally validate consistent sample-efficiency benefits through DF-ExpEnse across a variety of manipulation and locomotion tasks, compared to default finetuning and alternative action selection schemes. Project can be found at https://df-expense.github.io.

</details>


### [119] [Safe Local Navigation for Ackermann-Steered Robots in Unmapped Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19672)
*Christian Schaible,Shahin Sirouspour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对阿克曼转向移动机器人在无地图环境中的安全局部导航控制框架，通过凸二次优化确定最大障碍物间距的边界线，并用反馈线性化控制器跟踪参考路径。实验表明该方法比现有探索式规划器更安全且计算更高效，代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对无全局目标的未知环境中阿克曼转向移动机器人的安全局部导航问题，现有探索式规划器计算开销大且安全性不足。

Method: 通过局部障碍物检测确定最安全航向角（最大开阔空间方向），构建左右边界线（求解凸二次优化最大化车辆-障碍物间距），可选保持平行性和平滑性约束，利用反馈线性化控制器调节与边界线的距离实现局部参考路径跟踪。

Result: 实验表明该方法相比现有探索式规划器产生更安全的导航路径，且计算时间显著缩短。

Conclusion: 提出的控制框架在无地图环境中能有效实现阿克曼转向机器人的安全局部导航，兼顾安全性与计算效率，开源代码利于复现和应用。

Abstract: A control framework is proposed for safe local navigation of mobile robots equipped with Ackermann steering in unmapped environments where a global goal is absent. Based on local obstacle detections, the safest heading angle is determined along the direction of the largest open space ahead of the vehicle. Guided by this direction, bounding lines are constructed on the left and right sides of the vehicle to achieve obstacle separation. These bounding lines are obtained by solving a convex quadratic optimization that maximizes vehicle-to-obstacle clearance. Optionally, conditions are imposed on the bounding lines to preserve parallelism and smooth abrupt changes from prior control steps. A feedback-linearizing controller is then used to regulate the vehicle's distance from one or both bounding lines, effectively enabling tracking of a local reference path that preserves safety through obstacle clearance maximization. Open-source code is included for the application of this control scheme. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method produces safer navigation paths with significantly shorter computation times, compared to some existing exploration-based planners.

</details>


### [120] [ForEnt: A Multi-Modal Dataset for Characterizing Quadruped Robot Entrapments in Forest Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19675)
*Natapat Kirdwichai,Danesh Tarapore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForEnt是一个多模态数据集，包含在森林环境中使用Unitree Go2四足机器人采集的RGB-D图像、LiDAR扫描、本体感知数据和第三人称视频，记录了69次机器人腿部被植被缠绕（entrapment）的事件，旨在支持四足机器人在森林环境中的鲁棒部署研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在森林环境中执行生态监测任务时，经常因腿被藤蔓等植被缠绕而失去稳定性甚至翻倒，导致任务中断、需要人工干预并可能损坏硬件。当前缺乏专门的公开数据集来研究森林环境中的这类失效模式。

Method: 使用低成本的Unitree Go2四足机器人在英国Southampton Common Woodlands的8个森林站点采集数据，共进行11条序列、约1.7公里的遍历，记录69次缠绕事件。数据集包含时间同步的RGB-D图像、LiDAR扫描、本体感知数据和第三人称视频，并对缠绕事件进行了标注。

Result: 成功构建了首个专门针对森林环境中四足机器人腿部缠绕问题的多模态数据集，包含69次标注的缠绕事件，覆盖多种森林地形，为缠绕检测策略的评估提供了可复现的基准。

Conclusion: ForEnt数据集降低了开发在复杂森林环境中鲁棒的四足机器人部署方案的门槛，支持可重复的基准测试和缠绕检测方法的评估。

Abstract: Legged robots are increasingly deployed in forests for ecological surveying and monitoring, yet their autonomy is often interrupted consequent to the challenges posed in traversing forest environments. Forest entrapments, for example, when a robot's legs are ensnared in vines or other vegetation, result in loss of stability and toppling. Such events not only disrupt the mission and require manual intervention, but also risk damage to the robot hardware. To address the absence of a dedicated dataset to investigate these failure modes in forest environments, we present ForEnt, a multi-modal dataset collected with the low-cost Unitree Go2 quadruped across eight forest sites in the Southampton Common Woodlands, UK. For our dataset, over approximately 1.7 km of traversals in 11 sequences were conducted, yielding 69 recorded entrapment events. ForEnt includes time-synchronized RGB-D images, LiDAR scans, proprioceptive data, and third-person video, enabling analysis of terrain factors contributing to entrapment and providing labeled sensor streams for reproducible benchmarking. By supporting the evaluation of entrapment detection strategies, ForEnt lowers the barrier to developing robust quadruped robot deployments in challenging forest environments.

</details>


### [121] [Route-Constrained Robust Fusion Estimation for MEMS/GNSS Integrated Navigation of Unmanned Ground Vehicles in GNSS Degraded Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19687)
*Jingzhi Cui,Chao Zhang,Yuliang Mao,Shaolin Lü,Dongmei Li,Huan Che,Rong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对结构化道路环境中GNSS信号遮挡导致的无人地面车辆累积定位漂移问题，提出一种鲁棒的路径约束状态估计方法。通过将历史航迹推算轨迹与高清地图中的任务路段匹配，经二维刚性变换估计路径参考位置，作为伪位置观测融入扩展卡尔曼滤波，持续注入道路级约束以抑制位置偏差。引入触发控制、匹配质量验证、路径偏移补偿和单次更新限幅等工程策略。在长隧道、多段隧道和弯曲隧道实验中，有效抑制了卫星中断期间的误差累积，降低了最大偏差风险，提升了定位连续性和道路级可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无人地面车辆在结构化道路（如隧道）中GNSS信号严重遮挡时，航位推算累积漂移导致定位发散的问题，提升定位鲁棒性和连续性。

Method: 将历史航迹推算轨迹与高清地图任务路段进行匹配，通过二维刚性变换估计路径参考位置，将其作为伪位置观测融入扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）框架，实现道路级路径约束的持续注入；辅以触发控制、匹配质量验证、偏移补偿和单次修正限幅等工程策略。

Result: 在长隧道、多段隧道和弯曲隧道三种代表性场景中，该方法有效抑制了卫星信号中断期间的误差累积，降低了最大偏差风险，提升了定位连续性和道路级可用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的路径约束状态估计方法能够在GNSS信号长期中断的结构化道路环境中，通过道路级先验信息有效抑制定位漂移，具有工程实用价值。

Abstract: To address cumulative localization drift of unmanned ground vehicles in structured road environments under severe Global Navigation Satellite System signal occlusion, this paper proposes a robust route-constrained state estimation method. During periods without satellite signals, the proposed method establishes the correspondence between the historical dead reckoning trajectory and local segments of the mission route extracted from a high-definition map, and estimates a route-referenced position via a two-dimensional rigid transformation. The estimated position is then formulated as a pseudo-position observation and incorporated into an Extended Kalman Filter update. In this way, route constraints at the road level can be continuously injected into a unified state estimation framework, thereby suppressing position deviation relative to the mission route while indirectly improving azimuth estimation. To enhance practical applicability, engineering strategies, such as trigger control, matching quality validation, route offset compensation, and single update correction limiting, are further introduced. Experiments in three representative scenarios, including a long tunnel, a multi-segment tunnel, and a curved tunnel, show that the proposed method effectively suppresses error accumulation during satellite outages, reduces the risk of large maximum deviation, and improves localization continuity and road-level usability.

</details>


### [122] [Comparative Study on Agility, Efficiency, and Impact Absorption of Bipedal Robots with Active Toes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19699)
*Joong-Gil Kim,Wontae Ye,Geunwoo Cho,Seong-Ho Yun,Se-Hyoung Cho,Yong-Jae Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出14自由度仿人双足机器人，通过高保真仿真训练证明主动脚趾在行走中降低17.5%能耗、5.0%冲击力，并提升25-34%敏捷性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类脚趾对行走效率、敏捷性和减震至关重要，但现有机器人未充分模仿人类脚趾特性也缺乏严格量化验证。

Method: 设计14-DOF双足机器人模拟人类脚趾轻量高扭矩特性；构建高保真仿真环境（含实际传动和功耗）；使用最小化RL奖励函数进行公平对比（有/无主动脚趾）。

Result: 1.33m/s行走：主动脚趾减少17.5%运输成本（CoT）、降低5.0%足跟着地地面反作用力；敏捷性测试平均路径偏差降低25.0%、最大偏差降低34.0%。

Conclusion: 主动脚趾能显著提升双足机器人行走经济性与敏捷性，并有效吸收落地冲击。

Abstract: Human legs exhibit high efficiency, agility, and impact absorption, with toes playing a crucial role in these capabilities. While many attempts have been made to implement human-like toes in robots, they have not fully replicated human characteristics nor rigorously validated their benefits. We propose a 14-DOF biped robot emulating human toes' lightweight, high-torque, robust nature. To quantitatively analyze the effectiveness of the active toes in terms of agility, efficiency, and impact absorption, we developed a high-fidelity simulation training environment that reflects actual actuators with coupled transmissions and accurate power consumption. To ensure a fair comparison between configurations with and without active toes, we designed a minimal RL reward function and applied an identical training procedure to both. The simulation results indicate that, at 1.33 m/s walking, the toe-equipped robot reduced CoT by 17.5% and heel-strike GRF by 5.0% compared with the toe-ablation configuration. On the agility test, average and maximum path deviation decreased by 25.0% and 34.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [123] [A Differentiable Composite Approximation Framework for Autonomous Underwater Vehicle Maneuvering Modeling from Sea-Trial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19711)
*Aobo Wang,Aifei Xia,Zihao Wang,Lizhu Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种可微复合逼近方法，将多项式基函数与数据自适应基函数（神经网络）联合校准，用于基于实船数据的AUV操纵性建模，并引入转舵运动电流估计补偿机制处理海流影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于实船测量的AUV操纵性建模中，传统方法使用预定义多项式基函数，数据驱动方法使用数据自适应基函数，二者各自有局限。论文从基函数视角出发，希望联合两种方法的优势，同时解决现场数据中海流干扰问题。

Method: 提出可微复合逼近框架，将多项式基函数与神经网络残差作为单个预测器的可微部分联合校准；开发基于梯度的协同校准方法，引入敏感度感知机制约束多项式更新，神经网络捕获剩余非线性偏差；设计基于转舵运动的电流估计与补偿流程构建电流补偿学习目标。

Result: 使用7米长AUV在多种机动条件下的海试数据评估，该方法在递归轨迹和速度预测上优于纯多项式、纯神经网络以及冻结先验的混合基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效融合了传统模型与数据驱动模型的优势，且通过电流补偿提升了在真实海流环境中的预测性能，适用于基于实船数据的AUV操纵性建模。

Abstract: Field-based modeling from onboard measurements can produce autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV) maneuvering models that reflect real operating characteristics. From an approximation perspective, conventional maneuvering models use predefined constraint polynomial bases, whereas data-driven models use data-adaptive bases. Motivated by this basis-function view, this paper presents a differentiable composite-approximation formulation, in which the polynomial-basis component and the data-adaptive basis component are treated as differentiable parts of a single predictor and calibrated jointly. A gradient-based co-calibration method is developed for full-scale AUV maneuvering prediction, where a sensitivity-aware mechanism regulates bounded polynomial updates while the neural residual captures remaining nonlinear discrepancies under a shared prediction objective. To account for ocean-current effects in field data, a turning-motion-based current estimation and compensation procedure is incorporated to construct current-compensated learning targets for training and rollout. The framework is evaluated using sea-trial data collected from a 7-meter AUV under multiple maneuvering conditions. Results show that the proposed method improves recursive trajectory and velocity prediction compared with polynomial-only, neural-only, and frozen-prior hybrid baselines, demonstrating its applicability to field-data-based AUV maneuvering modeling.

</details>


### [124] [Bidirectional Tutoring for Developmental Motor Learning in Robots: Co-Developed Interaction Dynamics Support Stable Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19728)
*Rui Fukushima,Jun Tani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出双向教学（bidirectional tutoring）用于机器人运动技能学习，通过自由能原理扩展生成回放的神经网络实现逐序列稳定学习。在真实人形机器人的物体操作任务中，双向互动比单向教学产生更一致的行为模式和阶段性泛化能力，机器人逐渐减少对教师引导的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人运动技能学习多视为单向传递（被动接收示范），忽视了社交互动的双向性——教学者和学习者动态适应彼此。作者假设双向教学能利用先验约束指导一致行为模式的形成，而单向互动缺乏约束导致行为模式更分散。

Method: 基于自由能原理（Free Energy Principle）的神经网络扩展生成回放（Generative Replay），支持从单次教学剧集中逐序列稳定学习。在物理人形机器人（humanoid robot）上进行物体操作任务，设置两种实验：1）人类-机器人真实交互；2）AI教师通过自适应干预机制与真实机器人交互。

Result: 双向教学在两种实验设置下均催生了更一致的行为和阶段性泛化，机器人逐渐需要更少的教师引导；而单向互动缺少约束，行为模式更分散。

Conclusion: 双向教学作为一种具身且社会性扎根的方法，为机器人发展性运动学习提供了有效的支架。

Abstract: Infants are well known to develop their motor skills through dense interaction with caregivers. Although such social interaction is crucial for human development, motor-skill learning in robots is often treated as a unidirectional process in which robots passively receive demonstrations from tutors. This overlooks a key property of social interaction: it is inherently bidirectional, with tutor and learner dynamically adapting to each other. In such interactions, the robot's past experiences may function as prior constraints that shape the dynamics of their co-developed trajectories. We hypothesize that bidirectional tutoring allows such constraints to guide the formation of consistent behavioral patterns that preserve behavioral coherence and support generalization, whereas unidirectional interaction lacks such constraints and leads to broader, less consistent behavioral patterns. To examine this hypothesis, we conducted two experiments with a physical humanoid robot performing an object manipulation task: one involving human-robot interaction and another employing an AI tutor interacting with the real robot through an adaptive intervention mechanism designed to examine whether similar effects would emerge under more controlled conditions. We implement the developmental learning framework using a free-energy-principle-based neural network extended with generative replay, which supports stable sequence-by-sequence learning from single tutored episodes. Across both settings, bidirectional tutoring fostered consistent behaviors and stage-wise generalization, while the robot gradually required less tutor guidance. These results suggest that bidirectional tutoring, as an embodied and socially grounded approach, provides an effective scaffold for developmental motor learning in robots.

</details>


### [125] [VOiLA: Vectorized Online Planning with Learned Diffusion Model for POMDP Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19729)
*Marcus Hoerger,Rishikesh Joshi,Rahul Shome,Ian Manchester,Hanna Kurniawati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VOiLA框架利用条件扩散模型学习任务无关的POMDP模型（转移/观测采样器+观测似然模型），通过蒸馏为紧凑前馈生成器并集成GPU并行化在线规划器VOPP，在三个基准任务和实物机器人上实现了比Recurrent SAC更优的性能，采样成本降低近三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POMDP规划的实际应用受限于获取可靠POMDP模型的困难，现有方法难以在真实世界中高效学习任务无关的生成式模型用于在线规划。

Method: 1) 使用条件扩散模型学习转移和观测采样器，以及观测似然模型用于粒子滤波信念更新；2) 将扩散采样器蒸馏为紧凑前馈生成器以降低采样成本；3) 集成到支持GPU并行化的VOPP在线POMDP规划器中。

Result: 蒸馏策略将采样成本降低近三个数量级；在三个基准任务上使用不到10%的训练数据达到或优于Recurrent SAC的性能，对未见环境泛化能力更强；实物机器人10/10次成功完成任务（仅使用仿真数据训练）。

Conclusion: VOiLA展示了学习生成式POMDP模型用于在线规划的有效性和实用性，大幅降低采样成本，具备强泛化能力和sim-to-real迁移能力。

Abstract: Planning under uncertainty is an essential capability for autonomous robots. The Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) provides a powerful framework for such a capability. Although POMDP-based planning has advanced significantly, its application to real-world problems is often limited by the difficulty of obtaining faithful POMDP models. We present Vectorized Online planning wIth Learned diffusion model for POMDP Agents (VOiLA), a framework that learns task-agnostic POMDP models for online planning under uncertainty. VOiLA learns transition and observation samplers using conditional diffusion models and learns observation-likelihood models for particle-based belief updates. To enable efficient online planning, the diffusion samplers are distilled into compact feedforward generators and integrated with Vectorized Online POMDP Planner (VOPP), an online POMDP planner designed to leverage GPU parallelization. Experimental results indicate the distillation strategy reduces sampling cost by up to nearly three orders of magnitude, making learned generative POMDP models practical for online planning. Evaluation of VOiLA on three benchmark problems indicate that VOiLA achieves equal or better performance than Recurrent Soft Actor Critic while using less than 10% training data, and generalizes much better to unseen environment configurations. Physical robot evaluation indicates VOiLA uses the models learned using only simulated data and generates a policy that successfully accomplish the task in 10 of 10 runs.

</details>


### [126] [Temporal Self-Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19752)
*Yinsen Jia,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出时间自模仿学习(TSIL)框架，利用任务完成的时间效率作为自监督信号，通过挖掘高效成功轨迹并转化为可复用监督来优化长程机器人操作策略，在15个任务上显著提升学习效率和完成任务效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励塑造训练的长程机器人操作策略仍可能通过低效交互利用密集奖励，而高效的稀疏行为可能在训练中被遗忘，时间效率本身是未被充分利用的自监督来源。

Method: TSIL框架：从学习过程中挖掘时间高效的成功轨迹，基于配置条件的自适应时间目标逐步优化学习，通过效率加权自模仿学习保留和重放高效行为。

Result: 在15个不同的长程操作任务上，TSIL一致提升了学习效率、任务完成效率、对快速成功行为的重访率以及对不稳定训练条件的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 成功行为的时间结构本身提供了超越人工奖励塑造的可扩展自监督信号，时间效率是强化学习中强大且未被充分利用的监督源。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation policies trained with reward shaping can still exploit dense rewards through inefficient interaction, while rare efficient behaviors may be forgotten during training. We argue that temporal efficiency itself provides a powerful and underutilized source of self-supervision for reinforcement learning. We introduce Temporal Self-Imitation Learning (TSIL), a reinforcement learning framework that mines temporally efficient successful trajectories generated during learning and converts them into reusable supervision for future policy improvement. TSIL progressively refines learning using configuration-conditioned adaptive temporal targets derived from fast successful trajectories, while preserving and replaying efficient behaviors through efficiency-weighted self-imitation learning. Across 15 distinct long-horizon manipulation tasks, TSIL consistently improves learning efficiency, task-completion efficiency, revisitation of fast successful behaviors, and robustness to unstable training conditions. More broadly, our results suggest that the temporal structure of successful behavior itself provides a scalable self-supervisory signal for reinforcement learning beyond manually engineered reward shaping alone.

</details>


### [127] [Data Standards for Humanoid Robotics: The Missing Infrastructure for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19769)
*Shaoshan Liu,Xiugong Qin,Xuan Wu,Xuan Xia,Ning Ding,Jialu Liu,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文基于ISO/WD 26264-1标准制定工作，论证数据标准正成为具身智能的基础设施，提出人形机器人数据本质是具身交互数据，其价值依赖于物理一致性，主要瓶颈是非累积数据而非数据稀缺，标准应提供水平基础设施和领域语法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人的规模化不仅依赖模型和硬件，还需要物理经验能在机器人、任务、组织和时间维度上累积，但当前面临高采集成本、数据孤岛和评估不一致导致的非累积数据问题。

Method: 基于ISO/TC 299/WG 16工作组制定ISO/WD 26264-1标准的实践经验，从数据本质、物理一致性、瓶颈分析三个视角论证数据标准的必要性，并提出标准框架（水平基础设施+能力特定领域语法）。

Result: 提出三个关键洞察：1) 人形机器人数据是具身交互数据，需保留身体-动作-任务-场景-执行轨迹-结果的关系；2) 数据价值取决于时间、坐标、标定、运动学、单位和同步等物理一致性；3) 主要瓶颈是非累积数据。提出标准框架：水平层管理生命周期/元数据/溯源/质量/版本/可追溯性，能力层定义操作/移动/人机交互/认知等语法。

Conclusion: 数据标准应从组织数字信息演变为结构化物理交互的标准体系，成为Physical AI的基础设施。

Abstract: The scalability of humanoid robots will depend not only on models and hardware, but also on whether physical experience can accumulate across robots, tasks, organizations, and time. Drawing on the authors' work in developing ISO/WD 26264-1, Humanoid robot datasets -- Part 1: General requirements, within ISO/TC 299/WG 16, this article argues that data standards are becoming foundational infrastructure for Physical AI. We develop three insights. First, humanoid robot data is embodied interaction data, not a collection of isolated digital samples; a useful dataset must preserve the relationship among robot body, action, task, scene, execution trace, and outcome. Second, its value depends on physical coherence: multimodal streams are reusable only when timing, coordinate frames, calibration, kinematics, units, and synchronization assumptions remain inspectable. Third, the main bottleneck is not only data scarcity, but non-cumulative data caused by high collection costs, data silos, and inconsistent evaluation. We argue that humanoid robot data standards address these bottlenecks by making embodied experience interpretable, shareable, traceable, and reusable. A general standard should provide horizontal infrastructure for lifecycle management, metadata, provenance, quality, versioning, and traceability, while capability-specific parts should define domain grammar for manipulation, locomotion, human-robot interaction, cognition, and future humanoid capabilities. As AI moves from screens into bodies, data standards must evolve from organizing digital information to structuring physical interaction.

</details>


### [128] [Start Right, Arrive Right: Asynchronous Execution via Initial Noise Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19774)
*Trong-Bao Ho,Quang-Tan Nguyen,Thien-Loc Ha,Gia-Binh Nguyen,Viet-Thanh Nguyen,Long Dinh,Minh N. Vu,Duy M. H. Nguyen,An Thai Le,Ngo Anh Vien*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PAINT是一种无需训练的方法，通过选择合适的初始噪声而非修改生成过程，解决流式动作分块在异步执行时的边界不一致问题，在12个仿真和6个真实世界任务中提升了执行一致性和任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的动作分块（action chunking）在异步执行时，由于当前分块执行与下一分块生成之间存在延迟，导致分块边界处行为不一致（动作跳跃、不连贯）。现有方法需修改生成过程（steering），作者发现只需选择合适的初始噪声即可保持前缀一致性。

Method: 将异步推理重新定义为噪声选择问题：通过后向欧拉反演（backward Euler inversion）找到合适的初始噪声，使得未修改的流ODE生成的下一分块与已执行的动作前缀保持一致；通过重绘规则（repainting rule）构造最终分块。无需梯度、无需重新训练、无需修改策略。

Result: 在12个仿真基准任务和6个真实世界操作任务上，涵盖单臂、双臂和人形机器人，相比现有steering方法，显著提升了执行一致性和任务成功率。

Conclusion: 动作分块异步执行的不一致问题可以通过调整初始噪声解决，PAINT是训练自由、即插即用的高效方法，具有强实用性和跨本体泛化能力。

Abstract: Action chunking enables robot policies to produce temporally coherent behavior, but generating multi-step action sequences with flow-based policies incurs latency that is incompatible with real-time control. Under asynchronous execution, the robot continues executing the current chunk while the next one is generated, causing even minor delays to create inconsistencies at chunk boundaries. Existing methods address this problem by steering generation toward the already executed action prefix. We instead show that prefix consistency can be achieved by selecting an appropriate initial noise before generation begins, allowing the unmodified flow ODE to produce a coherent next chunk. This reframes asynchronous inference as a noise selection problem rather than a trajectory steering problem. We introduce \textbf{PAINT}, a training-free method that finds this noise via backward Euler inversion and constructs the final chunk through a repainting rule. In summary, \texttt{PAINT} requires no gradients, retraining, or policy modification; yet it improves execution consistency and task performance across \textit{12 simulated benchmarks} and \textit{6 real-world manipulation tasks} spanning single-arm, bimanual, and humanoid embodiments. Website: ~\href{https://paint-action-chunking.github.io}{\texttt{https://paint-action-chunking.github.io}}.

</details>


### [129] [EquiVLA: A General Framework for Rotationally Equivariant Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19784)
*Thien-Loc Ha,Quang-Tan Nguyen,Trong-Bao Ho,Long Dinh,Minh Duc Nguyen,Gia-Binh Nguyen,Pham Tri Quang,Minh N. Vu,Duy M. H. Nguyen,An Thai Le,Ngo Anh Vien*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EquiVLA 是首个端到端 SO(2)-等变 VLA 通用框架，通过 EquiPerceptor（近似等变视觉表征）和 EquiActor（精确等变扩散 Transformer 动作头）实现从观测到动作序列的 SO(2) 等变链，在 LIBERO、CALVIN 和真实机器人任务上显著提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型缺乏几何归纳偏置，在不同旋转配置下泛化需要大量数据，因此需要引入 SO(2) 等变性来提升数据效率和旋转泛化能力。

Method: 提出 EquiVLA 框架：(1) EquiPerceptor 从冻结的 ViT 特征中生成近似 SO(2)-等变视觉表征；(2) EquiActor 是基于 flow-matching 的精确 SO(2)-等变 Diffusion Transformer 动作头；两者构成从观测到动作的近似等变链。可适用于任何耦合冻结 VLM 骨干和扩散 Transformer 动作头的架构。

Result: LIBERO 四套件平均成功率 92.6%（基线 78.1%），CALVIN ABCD→D 平均序列长度 4.03（基线 3.45），真实机器人 Mobile ALOHA 成功率从 54% 提升至 72%。

Conclusion: EquiVLA 证明了在 VLA 中引入 SO(2) 几何等变性可以大幅提升旋转泛化能力和数据效率，且框架通用可适配不同 VLM 骨干，是等变 VLA 的首个通用方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for generalist robot manipulation, yet they lack geometric inductive biases: policies trained at specific orientations require substantially more data to generalize across rotational configurations. We present \textsc{EquiVLA}, the first general framework for end-to-end $\mathrm{SO}(2)$-equivariant VLA models, applicable to any architecture coupling a frozen vision-language backbone with a flow-matching Diffusion Transformer action head. \textsc{EquiVLA} introduces \textsc{EquiPerceptor}, which produces approximately $\mathrm{SO}(2)$-equivariant visual representations from frozen ViT features; and \textsc{EquiActor}, an exactly $\mathrm{SO}(2)$-equivariant flow-matching Diffusion Transformer action head. Together, they establish an approximate $\mathrm{SO}(2)$ equivariance chain from camera observations to predicted action sequences. Instantiated on GR00T~N1.5 and evaluated across four LIBERO suites, CALVIN ABCD$\to$D, and five real-robot tasks on Mobile ALOHA, \textsc{EquiVLA} achieves $92.6\%$ average success on LIBERO (vs. $78.1\%$ baseline), an average sequence length of $4.03$ on CALVIN (vs. $3.45$), and improves real-robot success from $54\%$ to $72\%$.

</details>


### [130] [TIDY: Thermal Infrared Image Denoising via Wavelet Domain Entropy and Directional Stripe Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19813)
*Tai Hyoung Rhee,Dong-Guw Lee,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TIDY，一种轻量级小波域热红外图像去噪方法，在真实室内噪声数据上训练，通过小波域分解显式分离噪声与结构内容，结合两种新损失函数（小波熵和小波方向条纹指数）抑制随机噪声和条纹伪影，在保证速度（~34Hz）的同时提升下游机器人任务（热惯性里程计、单目深度估计）的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热红外（TIR）成像在低光照条件下具有强感知能力，但受随机噪声和固定模式噪声严重影响，尤其在室内低热对比度环境下噪声加剧。现有去噪方法要么速度太慢无法用于机器人实时部署，要么对严重退化不鲁棒，且通常使用合成噪声训练。

Method: 提出轻量级小波域去噪器TIDY，在真实干净-噪声配对TIR数据上训练；将去噪问题转换到小波域，显式解耦噪声与结构内容以降低空间复杂度；引入两种新指标——小波熵（Wavelet Entropy）和小波方向条纹指数（Wavelet Directional Stripe Index）作为补充损失项，分别抑制随机噪声和条纹伪影。

Result: 推理速度达~34Hz（远超之前方法），在严重室内噪声和零样本设置下表现鲁棒，在热惯性里程计和单目深度估计等下游机器人任务中取得一致性能提升。代码和数据集已开源。

Conclusion: TIDY在效率与鲁棒性之间取得了优于现有方法的平衡，填补了室内TIR实时去噪的空白，且对实际机器人部署有直接推动作用。

Abstract: Thermal infrared (TIR) imaging has been a popular choice for field robotics due to its robust perception capability under low light visual degradation, but it suffers from severe stochastic and fixed-pattern noise that breaks downstream estimation. This noise is intensified indoors due to low thermal contrast and uniform temperature distributions, contributing to the relative lack of indoor TIR deployments. Existing TIR denoising methods exhibit a poor accuracy-efficiency tradeoff, either too slow for online deployment required in robotics or insufficiently robust to severe degradation, while typically being trained on synthetic noise. Addressing these problems, we propose TIDY, a lightweight wavelet-domain denoiser trained on real clean-noisy TIR data. By reformulating TIR denoising in the wavelet domain, TIDY explicitly disentangles noise from structural content, enabling targeted suppression with reduced spatial complexity, significantly improving inference speed over prior methods (~34Hz). TIDY introduces two new metrics, Wavelet Entropy and Wavelet Directional Stripe Index, as complementary loss terms to explicitly suppress stochastic noise and stripe artifacts. Across severe indoor corruption and zero-shot settings, TIDY improves robustness and yields consistent gains in downstream robotics tasks including thermal inertial odometry and monocular depth estimation. Code and dataset is available at: https://github.com/williamrheeth/TIDY

</details>


### [131] [World Engine: Towards the Era of Post-Training for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19836)
*Tianyu Li,Li Chen,Caojun Wang,Haochen Liu,Kashyap Chitta,Zhenjie Yang,Yuhang Lu,Naisheng Ye,Yihang Qiu,Yufei Wang,Luoxi Zou,Jiaxin Peng,Jin Pan,Zhaoyu Su,Andrei Bursuc,Shengbo Eben Li,Andreas Geiger,Peng Su,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: World Engine是一个生成式框架，从真实驾驶日志中重建高保真交互环境并系统外推为安全关键场景，用于对预训练驾驶模型进行后训练对齐，显著减少罕见安全关键场景下的碰撞事故。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶策略在常规场景表现良好，但受限于真实数据集中安全关键"长尾事件"稀缺，这些罕见交互定义了学习策略的实际安全边界却难以大规模收集，需要一种可扩展的方法来提升自动驾驶的安全性。

Method: World Engine从真实世界日志重建高保真交互环境，系统性地外推为现实的安全关键变体场景；通过强化学习对预训练驾驶模型进行后训练，使其对齐安全约束，规避真实世界探索的物理风险。

Result: 在nuPlan公开基准上显著减少罕见安全关键场景的失败；在量产级自动驾驶系统中减少模拟碰撞，并在实际道路测试中展现可测量的改进；效果远超仅扩展预训练数据。

Conclusion: 对合成安全关键交互进行后训练为更安全的自动驾驶提供了一条可扩展且有效的路径，代码已开源。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles must operate safely in the real world, where errors can have severe consequences. Although modern end-to-end driving policies excel in routine scenarios, their reliability is limited by the scarcity of safety-critical ``long-tail'' events in real driving datasets. These rare interactions define the practical safety boundary of the learned policy, yet they are difficult to collect at scale in the real world. Here we show that this fundamental limitation can be addressed by post-training pre-trained driving models on synthesized high-stakes interactions. We introduce World Engine, a generative framework that reconstructs high-fidelity interactive environments from real-world logs and systematically extrapolates them into realistic safety-critical variations. This paradigm enables reinforcement-based post-training to align policies with safety constraints, circumventing the physical risks inherent in real-world exploration. On a public benchmark built on nuPlan, World Engine substantially reduces failures in rare safety-critical scenarios and yields significantly larger gains than scaling pre-training data alone. Furthermore, when deployed on a production-scale autonomous driving system, the resulting policy reduces simulated collisions and demonstrates measurable improvements in on-road testing, showing that post-training on synthesized, safety-critical interactions offers a scalable and effective pathway to safer autonomous driving. The full codebase suite, including training, is released to the public.

</details>


### [132] [MMD-SLAM: Structure-Enhanced Multi-Meta Gaussian Distribution-Guided Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19874)
*Fan Zhu,Ziyu Chen,Peichen Liu,Yifan Zhao,Zhisong Xu,Hui Zhu,Hongxing Zhou,Sixun Liu,Chunmao Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MMD-SLAM是一个基于3DGS的视觉SLAM框架，引入亚特兰大世界假设指导的多元高斯表示，通过点线融合策略、结构先验编码和高斯进化策略，在跟踪精度和建图质量上达到SOTA，在ScanNet上ATE RMSE降低48.56%，在Replica上PSNR提升5.71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS-based SLAM系统未能充分利用场景底层结构信息，导致渲染质量受限且地图不一致。

Method: 提出三个创新：1) 点线融合策略用于位姿优化，引入3D线段提升跟踪鲁棒性；2) 基于亚特兰大世界假设的多元高斯表示，显式编码主导方向结构先验；3) 高斯进化策略自适应场景几何并将结构线索融入全局优化。

Result: 在ScanNet上ATE RMSE降低48.56%，在Replica上PSNR提升5.71%，跟踪精度和建图质量均达SOTA。

Conclusion: 结构增强的3DGS-SLAM框架在跟踪和建图方面显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has significantly boosted novel view synthesis and high-fidelity scene reconstruction, expanding the potential of 3DGS-based Visual Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) methods. However, most existing systems fail to fully exploit the underlying structural information, which limits rendering quality and often leads to inconsistent maps. To address these limitations, we propose MMD-SLAM, a structure-enhanced Visual SLAM framework that leverages the Atlanta World (AW) assumption to guide a Multi-Meta Gaussian representation for photorealistic mapping. First, we introduce a point-line fusion strategy for pose optimization, where 3D line segments are incorporated to improve tracking robustness and provide additional constraints for mapping. Second, we design a Multi-Meta Gaussian representation with dominant directions, explicitly encoding structural priors from the AW hypothesis. Finally, we propose a Gaussian evolution strategy that adapts to scene geometry and incorporates structural cues into global optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that these innovations enable MMD-SLAM to achieve state-of-the-art performance in both tracking accuracy and mapping quality. e.g., our method achieves a 48.56% reduction in ATE RMSE on ScanNet and a 5.71% improvement in PSNR on Replica, compared with MonoGS.

</details>


### [133] [One-to-Two Acting: A Novel Framework for Single-arm Agent Action Expansion to Dual Arms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19897)
*Youbin Yao,Nieqin Cao,Mingyan Li,Yan Ding,Fuqiang Gu,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ExS2D提出一种层次化动作扩展框架，仅使用单臂监督（零双臂演示）实现双臂操作。它通过结构化子任务生成、子任务引导的动作映射以及MLLM驱动的协调器进行优先级感知的动作分配与同步规划，在仿真中减少54.4%执行步骤且成功率与单臂基线相当，真实机器人四任务验证可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂操作可提升吞吐量，但收集双臂演示训练成本高且困难；现有方法依赖大量双臂数据，限制了可扩展性。

Method: 1) 从文本指令生成结构化子任务并捕捉时序优先级；2) 通过子任务引导将子任务映射为观测中的可执行动作；3) 基于多模态大语言模型的协调器进行优先级感知动作分配和同步规划，选择无碰撞双臂执行。

Result: 仿真实验平均执行步骤减少54.4%，成功率与单臂基线相当；真实机器人四任务中，在few-shot单臂样本下验证了双臂执行可靠性，零双臂演示数据。

Conclusion: ExS2D证明了仅从单臂监督即可实现有效的双臂操作，大幅降低数据需求，为双臂机器人学习提供了实用范式。

Abstract: Dual-arm manipulation can improve throughput via parallel execution, but collecting bimanual demonstrations for training is costly and difficult. We present ExS2D, a hierarchical action expansion framework that enables dual-arm manipulation from single-arm supervision. ExS2D first generates structured subtasks from textual instructions while explicitly capturing temporal precedence. It then grounds each subtask into executable actions through subtask-guided action mapping in observation. Finally, precedence-aware action allocation and synchronized planning are performed by a multimodal large language model driven coordinator to select collision-free dual-arm executions. Simulation experiments demonstrate that ExS2D reduces the average execution steps by 54.4% while maintaining a comparable success rate to a single-arm baseline. Real-robot experiments on four tasks further demonstrate the reliability of ExS2D for dual-arm execution under few-shot single-arm samples, while using zero bimanual demonstrations.

</details>


### [134] [Co-policy: Responsive Human-Robot Co-Creation for Musical Performances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19914)
*Xuetao Li,Wenke Huang,Mang Ye,Zijian Liu,Jinhua Xie,Jifeng Xuan,Miao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Co-policy框架，将人类-机器人音乐共创分解为语义意图接地、约束音乐变体和视觉运动执行三个模块，使用微调的Qwen-vl规划器（F-Qwen）和Gaussian-Mixture Visuomotor Policy（GMP）实现低延迟的物理音乐合奏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人音乐共创需要将语义音乐理解与实时物理可执行表演连接起来，现有方法难以兼顾语义理解、实时性和物理执行约束。

Method: Co-policy框架：1) F-Qwen规划器将语音、音乐种子和视觉观察转化为结构化共创计划；2) 条件混合密度策略GMP将目标音符和视觉上下文映射到多模态机器人动作，单次前向传播完成低延迟执行。

Result: 真实机器人编钟实验中，相比diffusion-policy和消融基线，在意图对齐、执行准确性和响应频率方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 物理接地动作生成是具身人机共创的关键要求，Co-policy有效结合语义理解与物理执行。

Abstract: Art has long stood as a pivotal expression of human creativity. Embodied artificial intelligence offers a route for generative models to participate in that creativity through physical action rather than disembodied digital content. In robotic music co-creation, it is challenging to connect semantic musical understanding with real-time and physically executable performance. We present Co-policy, a framework for human-robot musical co-creation that separates semantic intent grounding, constrained musical variation, and visuomotor execution. To ground musical semantics, Co-policy uses pre-inference semantic anchors and a fine-tuned Qwen-vl planner (F-Qwen) to transform speech, live musical seeds, and visual observations into structured co-creation plans. To support low-latency execution, Co-policy introduces a Gaussian-Mixture Visuomotor Policy (GMP), implemented as a conditional mixture-density policy that maps target notes and visual context to multimodal robot actions in a single forward pass. Unlike robotic playback systems that merely reproduce user-specified notes, Co-policy generates complementary musical responses under both musical and physical constraints. Real-robot chime experiments, ablations, and expert evaluation show improved intent alignment, execution accuracy, and response frequency over diffusion-policy and ablated baselines, supporting physically grounded action generation as a key requirement for embodied human-AI co-creation.

</details>


### [135] [Deep-Unfolded Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19920)
*Hunter Kuperman,Minchan Jung,Rahul V. Ghosh,Alex Oshin,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Deep Coordinator，一种深度展开框架，在求解时动态调整ADMM-DDP分布式优化器的超参数，实现6.18-9.44倍加速且可泛化至8倍大规模系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式优化在多机器人问题中可扩展且透明，但严重依赖问题特定的超参数手动调优，缺乏自适应能力。

Method: 将固定次数的ADMM-DDP迭代展开为神经网络，层间通过可学习函数将优化器状态映射为下一迭代的超参数；并提出无监督学习方案避免监督学习导致的退化解。

Result: 在车队和四旋翼仿真中，轨迹质量相当的情况下比传统求解器快6.18-9.44倍，且可在未训练的8倍大规模系统上保持性能优势。

Conclusion: 深度展开框架可有效实现分布式优化器的运行时自适应超参数调整，显著加速并具有良好的规模泛化性。

Abstract: Distributed optimization is a highly scalable and structurally transparent technique to solve multi-agent robotics problems; however, such methods often suffer from the need for highly-specialized, problem-specific hyperparameter tunings. In this work, we propose Deep Coordinator, a deep-unfolding framework that learns to dynamically adjust the hyperparameters of ADMM-DDP, a popular distributed solver for robotics tasks, at solve-time in response to optimizer performance. Our architecture consists of unrolling a fixed number of ADMM-DDP iterations into a neural network with learnable functions between layers mapping the optimizer state to the next hyperparameters. To the best of our knowledge, Deep Coordinator is the first deep-unfolding framework to adapt the penalty parameters of a non-convex optimizer at solve-time; we show that the mainstream supervised approach can yield degenerate solutions when training such models, and propose an unsupervised learning scheme. On simulations with fleets of cars and quadrotors, Deep Coordinator produces trajectories of comparable quality 6.18-9.44x faster than conventional solvers. Furthermore, Deep Coordinator retains its performance benefits when deployed to systems up to 8x larger than trained on.

</details>


### [136] [SWAP: Symmetric Equivariant World-Model for Agile Robot Parkour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19928)
*Kaixin Lan,Ze Wang,Hongyi Li,Lei Jiang,Chaojie Fu,Chengkai Su,Choi Lam Wong,Yongbin Jin,Hongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SWAP，一种端到端等变对称世界模型，将对称性嵌入到世界模型和Actor-Critic网络中，让四足机器人实现2.13米跳跃和1.63米攀爬的越野记录，并具备对未见镜像地形的几何泛化能力和零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的隐式世界模型会冗余编码左右对称的交互模式，增加学习负担并阻碍几何规律捕获，限制了隐空间对下游策略的效率。

Method: 提出端到端等变对称世界模型SWAP，将对称性（镜面对称等变）直接嵌入到世界模型和Actor-Critic网络中，作为结构先验引导学习。

Result: 四足机器人在真实世界中跨越2.13米间隙、攀爬1.63米平台，打破越野纪录；对未见镜像地形有几何泛化能力，在多样户外环境中具备零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: 对称等变性是一种有效的结构先验，能够推动学习型腿式运动在物理极限上的突破。

Abstract: While latent world models enable the proactive predictions required for extreme parkour, their purely data-driven nature forces them to redundantly encode left-right symmetric interactions as independent patterns. This inflates the learning burden and hinders the capture of geometric regularities, restricting the latent space's efficiency for downstream policies. To address this, we propose SWAP, an end-to-end equivariant symmetric world model. This framework embeds symmetry directly into both the world model and the actor-critic networks. In real-world tests, the robot leaps across a 2.13 m gap and climbs a 1.63 m platform, breaking records for quadruped parkour. Furthermore, the framework exhibits robust geometric generalization to unseen mirrored terrains and exceptional zero-shot transferability across diverse outdoor environments. These results demonstrate that symmetry equivariance is an effective structural prior for pushing the physical boundaries of learned legged locomotion.

</details>


### [137] [Motor Angular Speed Preintegration for Multirotor UAV State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19929)
*Matěj Petrlík,Filip Novák,Robert Pěnička,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于电机转速预积分的MAS-LO算法，替代传统IMU进行状态传播，在无人机状态估计中相比LIO-SAM位置精度提升28%、速度精度提升65%、延迟降低14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机IMU受螺旋桨振动干扰，导致状态估计精度下降；现有方法依赖融合慢速位姿与高频惯性测量，但振动影响难以消除。

Method: 利用电机转速获取加速度，取代传统IMU进行状态传播；提出预积分电机转速因子，可直接用于因子图优化；结合LiDAR测量形成MAS-LO算法。

Result: 相比LIO-SAM：位置精度提升28%、速度精度提升65%、测量延迟降低14%、对参数错误鲁棒性高。

Conclusion: 电机转速预积分可有效替代IMU用于无人机状态估计，在精度和鲁棒性上均优于传统惯性方法。

Abstract: A precise state estimate is crucial for a tight feedback control that enables agile and near-obstacle flights of UAVs. The state-of-the-art methods fuse slow pose measurements with high-frequency inertial measurements to obtain a precise state estimate. However, the inertial measurements from the IMU onboard the UAV are degraded by vibrations from spinning propellers and the precision of the estimated state suffers. We propose a novel approach based on the preintegration of accelerations obtained from motor speeds. We show that the accelerations obtained in this manner can be used for state propagation on their own to achieve better precision without including the IMU. Further, we propose a factor composed of the preintegrated motor speeds that can be directly employed in factor graph optimization frameworks. We combine our factor with LiDAR measurements into the proposed Motor Angular Speed LiDAR Odometry (MAS-LO) algorithm for precise state estimation, which we open-source. Lastly, we evaluate the estimation precision against a state-of-the-art inertial algorithm LIO-SAM to show 28% improvement in position and 65% in velocity estimation accuracy, 14% lower measurement lag, and high robustness to wrong parameter values.

</details>


### [138] [Evaluation of Augmented Reality-based Intuitive Interface for Robot-Assisted Transesophageal Echocardiography: A User Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19971)
*Xiu Zhang*,Matteo Di Mauro*,Sofia Breschi,Angela Peloso,Emiliano Votta,Arianna Menciassi,Elena De Momi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种基于增强现实(AR)的直观界面用于机器人辅助经食管超声心动图(TEE)，通过对比2D/3D可视化及关节级/尖端级交互方式，发现3D可视化+尖端级控制(3D-TI)在定位精度(位置误差从13mm降至3mm)和操作负荷方面最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动TEE操作要求高、体力消耗大且伴随辐射暴露，现有机器人辅助TEE系统缺乏直观有效的用户界面，亟需设计能提升空间感知和控制直观性的交互方案。

Method: 构建机器人TEE平台，集成电磁追踪和虚拟模拟器，设计三种界面(2D关节级/3D关节级/3D尖端级)；36名受试者执行标准化导航任务，量化评估位置/方向误差、完成时间和NASA-TLX工作负荷。

Result: 3D可视化将中位位置误差从13mm降至3mm，方向误差减半；尖端级交互进一步降低50%方向误差并减少个体差异；3D-TI配置在准确性和人机工效上均最优。

Conclusion: AR可视化与尖端级直观控制可显著提升机器人TEE系统的操作性能和安全性，为下一代系统设计提供依据。

Abstract: TransEsophageal Echocardiography (TEE) is essential for diagnosing and guiding Structural Heart Disease (SHD) interventions. However, manual TEE manipulation demands significant operator expertise, is physically demanding, and exposes clinicians to radiation when performed alongside fluoroscopy. Robotic-assisted TEE systems have been introduced to improve probe handling and reduce operator fatigue, yet the design of intuitive and effective user interfaces remains an open challenge. This study presents and evaluates a model-enhanced, Augmented Reality (AR)-based intuitive interface for robot-assisted TEE, designed to improve spatial awareness and control intuitiveness. A robotic TEE platform integrated with electromagnetic tracking and a virtual simulator was used to compare three user interfaces differing in visualization and interaction modalities: 2D jointlevel (2D-JI), 3D joint-level (3D-JI), and 3D tip-level (3D-TI). Thirty six participants performed standardized navigation tasks to reproduce target echocardiographic views, with performance assessed via position and orientation errors, completion time, and NASA-TLX workload scores. Results show that 3D visualization significantly improved spatial accuracy, reducing median position error from 13 mm to 3 mm and halving the orientation error compared with the 2D interface. Tip-level interaction yielded a further 50% reduction in orientation error and reduced interuser variability relative to joint-level control. Overall, the 3D-TI configuration, combining immersive visualization with direct tip-level control, proved the most effective and ergonomic interface, supporting the integration of AR-based visualization and intuitive control paradigms into next-generation robotic TEE systems to enhance operator performance and procedural safety.

</details>


### [139] [Tri-Info: Generalizable, Interpretable Failure Prediction for VLA Models via Information Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19998)
*Jinghan Yang,Yunchao Zhang,Wang Yuan,Haolun Wan,Jiaming Zhang,Zhengyang Hu,Yanchao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Tri-Info信息论信号用于VLA模型的失败检测，通过衡量动作多样性、时间一致性和状态转移耦合度，在不需重训练的情况下跨架构、环境和sim-to-real迁移，在真实任务上达到83%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在物理交互中可能造成不可逆伤害，现有黑箱模型缺乏可泛化且可解释的失败检测方法。

Method: 将VLA控制形式化为闭环信息管道，推导出三个信息论信号（Tri-Info）：动作多样性、时间一致性、状态转移耦合度，用于捕获成功与失败rollout的系统性信息差异。

Result: 在6个VLA模型和3个基准环境中，Tri-Info达到与最强基线相当的性能；跨架构、环境和sim-to-real迁移无需重训练，在真实任务上达到83%准确率，而基线方法退化为随机水平。

Conclusion: Tri-Info是一种简单而强大的方法，不仅具备强跨域泛化的失败检测能力，还能提供底层失败模式的可解释诊断。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are increasingly deployed across diverse tasks, yet they remain black boxes whose physical interactions can cause irreversible harm, making generalizable and interpretable failure detection essential. We observe that successful and failed rollouts carry systematically different information-theoretic signatures. Building on this, we formalize VLA control as a closed-loop information pipeline and derive the Triple Information-theoretic (Tri-Info) signals that capture whether actions remain diverse, temporally consistent, and coupled to state transitions. Across six VLA models and three benchmark environments, Tri-Info matches the strongest baselines in-domain. Moreover, Tri-Info transfers across architectures, environments, and the sim-to-real gap without retraining, reaching 83\% accuracy on real-world tasks where prior detectors collapse to chance. This establishes Tri-Info as a simple yet powerful method that not only detects failures with strong cross-domain generalization, but also delivers interpretable diagnostics of the underlying failure modes.

</details>


### [140] [A Neuromorphic Reinforcement Learning Framework for Efficient Pathfinding in Robotic Mobile Fulfillment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20031)
*Junzhe Xu,Zecui Zeng,Lusong Li,Yuetong Fang,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SDQN-RMFS框架，将强化学习训练的路径规划策略从全精度ANN通过知识蒸馏转换为SNN，部署到神经形态芯片上，实现RMFS中超低功耗、低延迟的路径规划。硬件实验相比GPU实现11,281倍能耗节约和近两倍延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境、受限空间和严格实时约束使传统搜索和规则方法在RMFS路径规划中面临高计算复杂度和长决策延迟；RL虽有效但将其部署到资源受限硬件上仍具挑战。

Method: 全栈流水线：1) 碰撞允许策略训练ANN策略以稠密化信息轨迹；2) 通过硬标签知识蒸馏将ANN转换为SNN，解决输出分布不匹配问题；3) 部署到神经形态芯片，基于稀疏事件触发计算。

Result: 相比高性能GPU基线，实现11,281倍能耗节约、近两倍延迟降低，决策质量与原始训练策略持平。

Conclusion: 物理神经形态推理可作为大规模RMFS运营中实用且能源可持续的路径。

Abstract: Dynamic environmental changes, confined workspaces, and stringent real-time constraints make pathfinding in Robotic Mobile Fulfillment Systems (RMFS) a challenging problem for conventional search- and rule-based methods, which typically suffer from high computational complexity and long decision latency. While reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful alternative, deploying learned policies with extreme energy efficiency on resource-constrained hardware remains an open challenge. We present SDQN-RMFS, an end-to-end framework that achieves high-fidelity deployment of an RL-trained policy from a full-precision artificial neural network (ANN) through to a neuromorphic chip. By computing only when triggered by sparse events, this framework unlocks ultra-low-power RMFS pathfinding. Our full-stack pipeline operates as follows: an ANN policy is first efficiently trained via a collision-allowing strategy to densify informative trajectories, and then converted into a spiking neural network (SNN) via a hard-label knowledge distillation approach. This effectively addresses the output distribution mismatch, preserving policy capability across the ANN-to-SNN pipeline while substantially reducing inference latency. Hardware experiments demonstrate up to 11,281$\times$ energy savings and a nearly two-fold reduction in latency compared to a high-performance GPU baseline, while maintaining decision quality on par with the original trained policy. These results establish physical neuromorphic inference as a practical and energy-sustainable pathway for large-scale RMFS operations.

</details>


### [141] [MirrorDuo: Reflection-Consistent Visuomotor Learning from Mirrored Demonstration Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20048)
*Zheyu Zhuang,Ruiyu Wang,Giovanni Luca Marchetti,Florian T. Pokorny,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MirrorDuo 提出基于镜像反射的数据增强方法，通过对图像、本体感知和6-DoF末端执行器动作进行对称变换，实现"采集一个，免费获得一个"的效果，可作为行为克隆和扩散策略的即插即用增强策略或反射等变网络的结构先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图像的行为克隆受限于采集多样化演示数据的成本，尤其是在跨工作空间变化时泛化困难。现有方法需要大量人工采集数据，效率低下。

Method: 对每个原始演示生成对应的镜像副本，操作对象为图像、本体感知和6-DoF末端执行器动作三元组。可作为数据增强策略应用于标准行为克隆或扩散策略，也可作为反射等变策略网络的结构先验。

Result: 在相同数据预算下，当演示均匀分布在工作空间两侧时性能显著提升；当演示仅限一侧时，仅需0-5个目标配置演示即可实现高效技能迁移到镜像工作空间。

Conclusion: MirrorDuo是一种简洁高效的反射式数据增强方法，能有效缓解演示数据采集成本问题，提升技能泛化能力。

Abstract: Image-based behaviour cloning leverages demonstrations captured from ubiquitous RGB cameras. However, it remains constrained by the cost of collecting diverse demos, especially for generalizing across workspace variations. We propose MirrorDuo, a reflection-based formulation that operates on image, proprioception, and full 6-DoF end-effector action tuples, generating a mirrored counterpart for each original demonstration, effectively achieving "collect one, get one for free". It can be applied as a data augmentation strategy for existing learning pipelines, such as standard behaviour cloning or diffusion policy, or as a structural prior for reflection-equivariant policy networks. By leveraging the overlap between the original and mirrored domains, MirrorDuo achieves significantly improved performance under the same data budget when demonstrations are evenly distributed across both sides of the workspace. When demonstrations are confined to one side, MirrorDuo enables efficient skill transfer to the mirrored workspace with as few as zero or five demos in the target arrangement.

</details>


### [142] [VFILC: Accurate Frequency Extrapolations in Imitation Learning via Sampling Frequency ILC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20056)
*Nozomu Masuya,Toshiaki Tsuji,Sho Sakaino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可变频率模仿学习与迭代学习控制（VFILC）结合方法，通过前馈（VFIL）+反馈（ILC）结构实现外推高速运动时的频率误差校正，在擦洗、摇晃、混合三项任务中验证了速度外推精度，频率误差最高降低81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NN模仿学习方法在变速度运动中，插值速度受限，外推超出训练范围的速度时会产生不可预测运动；VFIL通过连接采样频率与运动频率实现外推，但开环配置导致频率误差，尤其在高频外推场景下误差严重。

Method: 提出VFILC（可变频率模仿学习+迭代学习控制），包含前馈部分（VFIL生成参考轨迹）和反馈部分（ILC迭代校正频率误差），两者结合实现速度外推与误差补偿。

Result: 三项任务均成功外推运动速度并降低频率误差：擦洗任务频率误差降低81%，摇晃任务降低50%（均相较简单前馈VFIL，双倍平均速度外推）；在受复杂摩擦影响的接触式混合任务中，即使在插值频率下也比VFIL准确率提升27%。

Conclusion: VFILC通过前馈+反馈架构有效解决了VFIL开环频率误差问题，实现了准确的速度外推，尤其在高频外推场景下效果显著。

Abstract: Conventional neural network (NN)-based imitation learning methods for variable-speed motion either restricted their scope to interpolated speeds, or generated unpredictable motions when extrapolating beyond trained velocity ranges. Variable-frequency imitation learning (VFIL) enabled extrapolations of speeds by linking the NN model's sampling frequency to the motion frequency, whereas its open-loop configuration caused frequency errors, especially in the extrapolated high-frequency settings. This study proposes variable-frequency imitation learning with iterative learning control (VFILC) based on a combination of VFIL and iterative learning control (ILC) with both feedforward and feedback parts, the former taking advantage of VFIL and the latter adjusting the frequency errors. The experimental results showed that the proposed method successfully and accurately extrapolated motion speeds and reduced frequency errors in all three tasks, and that the feedback especially reduced the frequency errors by a remarkable 81% in the wiping task and 50% in the shaking task, both compared to simple feedforward VFIL, when extrapolating at double the average speed in the training data. The proposed method also improved accuracy by 27% compared with VFIL even at an interpolated frequency for a contact-rich mixing task affected by complex friction traits.

</details>


### [143] [Pose6DAug: Physically Plausible Multi-view Object Swapping for Robot Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20118)
*Jonghoon Lee,Seong Hyeon Park,Byungwoo Jeon,Minha Lee,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Pose6DAug，一种基于失败驱动的数据增强框架，通过利用策略自身成功轨迹中的6D位姿信息，将目标物体替换为失败案例中的新物体，生成多视图几何一致的物理合理演示数据，无需额外数据采集即可提升VLA策略对新物体的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在分布外新物体上泛化失败，传统收集多视图遥操作数据扩展性差，成本高。

Method: 从成功轨迹中提取6D位姿轨迹，将目标物体替换为新物体网格，利用位姿驱动的显式网格渲染生成多视图一致的新演示数据，用于微调VLA策略。

Result: 在新物体上相比SOTA基线提升16.5%的成功率，同时保持分布内性能不降。

Conclusion: 多视图物理一致的增强是实现VLA可扩展泛化的实用路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies have shown strong potential for general-purpose manipulation, yet they often fail on novel, out-of-distribution objects whose appearance or geometry deviates from the training distribution. The standard remedy is to collect multi-view teleoperation data for every failure case, but this scales poorly in both cost and time. We introduce Pose6DAug, a failure-driven data augmentation framework that turns a policy's own successful episodes into targeted demonstrations for its failure modes, without any new data collection. Our key insight is that each successful episode already encodes a physically valid action trajectory together with calibrated multi-view observations. By swapping only the manipulated object while preserving this trajectory, we obtain new and physically grounded demonstrations. However, naive 2D video editing breaks multi-view consistency and physical plausibility, particularly under heavy occlusion and egocentric viewpoints. Our method instead operates directly in 3D, anchoring the target object with an explicit mesh driven by a temporally coherent 6D pose trajectory, ensuring geometrically consistent renderings across all camera views. Fine-tuning a VLA on data augmented by our method improves success rates by 16.5% relative to the state-of-the-art baseline on novel objects, while preserving in-distribution performance. These results show that multi-view and physically consistent augmentation is a practical path to scalable VLA generalization.

</details>


### [144] [Dual-Agent Framework for Cross-Model Verified Translation of Natural-Language Protocols into Robotic Laboratory Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20120)
*Hyeonna Choi,Jung Yup Kim,Hyuneui Lim,Seunggyu Jeon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于智能体的协议翻译框架，将自然语言描述的微孔板实验协议自动转换为机器人实验平台可执行的控制命令，包含解析器、规则映射引擎和LLM验证智能体，并在ELISA和Bradford实验中验证了端到端自主执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言撰写的生物实验协议与自动化系统所需的控制命令之间存在语义鸿沟，限制实验的自主执行。微孔板自动实验需要同时控制孔映射、样本-试剂组合、重复放置和并行分液，难度更大。

Method: 1) Parser Agent将自然语言协议形式化为结构化表示；2) 基于规则的映射引擎将操作约束转化为设备级控制命令；3) 异构LLM验证智能体检查完整性、参数精度和执行顺序，检测错误时触发带结构化反馈的自校正循环。

Result: 在ELISA协议上评估了7种Parser和3种Validator的翻译准确率和通过率；比较了规则映射与LLM端到端映射的精度-延迟权衡；在机器人平台上成功实现了Bradford法蛋白定量实验的端到端自主执行。

Conclusion: 提出的框架为缩小自然语言协议与微孔板自驱动实验室之间的语义鸿沟提供了一种灵活的方法。

Abstract: Biological experiment protocols are written in natural language, whereas automation systems rely on predefined control commands, creating a semantic gap that limits autonomous execution. Microplate-based automatic experiments are particularly challenging due to the need to simultaneously control well mapping, sample-reagent combinations, replicate placement, and parallel dispensing. This study proposes an agent-based protocol translation framework that converts natural-language microplate-based protocols into executable control commands for a robotic laboratory platform. A Parser Agent formalizes the natural-language protocol into a structured representation, and a rule-based mapping engine deterministically incorporates the operational constraints of the robotic laboratory platform to generate device-level control commands. A heterogeneous LLM Validation Agent verifies completeness, parameter accuracy, and execution order, and triggers a self-correction loop with structured feedback when errors are detected. A sweep involving 7 Parsers and 3 Validators on randomly selected ELISA protocols evaluates how model scale and Validator type affect translation accuracy and pass rates under cross-model verification. The accuracy-latency trade-off is further verified by comparing the rule-based mapping of the proposed framework with LLM end-to-end direct mapping. Finally, Bradford assay-based protein quantification using a microplate was demonstrated on a robotic laboratory platform, validating end-to-end autonomous execution from natural-language protocols to real-world experiments. The proposed framework provides a flexible approach to narrowing the semantic gap between natural-language protocols and microplate-based self-driving laboratories.

</details>


### [145] [Frequency-Aware Flow Matching for Continuous and Consistent Robotic Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20135)
*Jianing Guo,Fangzheng Chen,Zihao Mao,Wong Lik Hang Kenny,Zhenhong Wu,Yu Li,Yishuai Cai,Yuanpei Chen,Yikun Ban,Kai Chen,Qi Dou,Yaodong Yang,Xianglong Liu,Huijie Zhao,Simin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出频率感知流匹配（FAFM），通过离散余弦变换将离散动作序列转换到频域进行流匹配，再重建连续动作，并加入Sobolev型平滑正则化，实现连续、时间一致的动作生成，无需额外参数，兼容多种策略框架，在仿真和真实机器人上均取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配策略依赖离散化动作块，对异构控制频率的演示数据鲁棒性差，且易产生时间不一致的动作，影响控制稳定性。

Method: 1) 使用DCT将离散动作序列变换到频域系数，在频域上做流匹配，再通过余弦基展开重建连续动作，以处理异构频率输入；2) 对一阶时间导数进行正则化（Sobolev约束），抑制高频误差和突变，生成时间一致的动作。

Result: 在合成玩具基准、避障、LapGym、LIBERO及真实Franka机器人上，FAFM在成功率、多模态表达能力、运动平滑性、收敛速度、对机械偏差和混合频率输入的鲁棒性方面均优于基线。

Conclusion: FAFM是一种简洁有效的连续时间动作生成方法，不增加网络参数，可即插即用于独立流匹配策略和视觉-语言动作模型，提升控制稳定性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Flow matching has emerged as a standard paradigm for robotic manipulation owing to its strong expressive power for modelling complex, multimodal action distributions, alongside similar approaches like diffusion policy. However, existing methods rely on discretized action chunks, making them brittle to demonstrations collected at heterogeneous control frequencies and prone to temporally inconsistent actions that degrade control stability. In this paper, we propose Frequency-Aware Flow Matching (FAFM), which outputs continuous, temporally consistent actions. To handle heterogeneous frequency input, we transform discrete action sequences into the frequency domain with the discrete cosine transform (DCT), perform flow matching over the resulting coefficients, and reconstruct continuous actions via cosine basis expansion. To generate temporally consistent actions, we regularize the first-order temporal derivative to promote smooth actions. This corresponds to a Sobolev-type constraint that suppresses high-frequency errors and discourages abrupt action changes. Our FAFM is simple, introduces no additional network parameters and applies to standalone flow-matching policies and vision-language action models. Across synthetic toy benchmark, obstacle avoidance, LapGym, and LIBERO, FAFM improves success rates, multimodal expressivity, motion smoothness, convergence speed, robustness to mechanical bias and mixed-frequency input. These gains are consistent when deployed on a real-world Franka robot. Code available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/FAFM.

</details>


### [146] [Robust Assembly State Reasoning from Action Recognition for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20150)
*James Fant-Male,Roel Pieters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 系统研究并比较了基于动作识别的装配状态跟踪方法，发现逻辑方法、HMM和NN在不同任务中表现各异，无统一最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HAR在HRC中被广泛研究，但如何利用HAR精确跟踪装配状态尚未充分探索，且在实际场景中并非易事。

Method: 使用两个数据集和五种状态跟踪方法（逻辑法、HMM、NN），在模拟噪声输入和真实HAR输入下进行系统比较。

Result: NN和HMM在有限变异性任务中表现良好，但在其他场景下逻辑方法更鲁棒；对重复动作任务，建模预期动作持续时间很重要。

Conclusion: 最优跟踪方法因任务而异，不同方法在不同情况下失效，需根据任务特性选择合适方法。

Abstract: Human Action Recognition (HAR) is frequently investigated in Human-Robot Collaboration (HRC) research to understand what actions have been performed and hence the state of a collaborative task. Accurately tracking an assembly state from HAR is however not fully investigated, and in realistic scenarios is not a trivial task. This research systematically investigates and compares methods for tracking assembly state using action recognition inputs. Investigations using two diverse datasets and five state tracking approaches, including logic-based, Hidden Markov Model (HMM), and neural network (NN) methods, show that optimal approaches are not uniform across different tasks and that different methods fail under different circumstances. Testing is performed using both simulated inputs with varying noise levels and realistic inputs from a HAR model. Results show NN and HMM methods can perform well in tasks with limited variability, but for other scenarios logic-based approaches can be more robust. Methods which model expected action duration are also important for tasks with repeated actions where no additional sensing is provided.

</details>


### [147] [Belt-Finger: An Affordable Soft Belt-Driven Gripper for Dexterous In-Hand Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20193)
*Boya Zhang,Andreas Zell,Georg Martius*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种双软带手指模块，为平行夹爪增加平移、俯仰、滚转三个自由度，在保持简单可靠的同时大幅提升灵巧操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统平行夹爪虽简单可靠，但缺乏手中灵活性，导致大范围机械臂运动和受限空间内灵巧操作困难。

Method: 设计双软带手指模块实现三自由度手中操作，集成到MPC控制器和轻量级遥操作接口（10自由度）中，支持已知物体操作和人机协同控制。

Result: 在遥操作、MPC和训练策略下，相比传统平行夹爪，所提夹爪在多项挑战性任务中持续提升灵巧性和任务可行性。

Conclusion: 通过简单的机械升级即可为平行夹爪增加三个关键手中自由度，兼顾低成本制造与可靠控制，显著拓宽操作范围。

Abstract: Parallel-jaw grippers are the default manipulator choice in robotics because they are simple, robust, and inexpensive. Their limited in-hand mobility, however, often forces large arm motions and restricts dexterous manipulation in confined workspaces.
